					holder_scope = { 
						opinion = {
							modifier = opinion_joined_rebellion

						}

				}
				d_dragonstone = {
					holder_scope = { 
						opinion = {
							who = ROOT

							who = ROOT
							years = 100
						}

							join_attacker_wars = PREV

		}

			force_mult = 1
		}

				limit = { NOT = { start_date = 8131.1.8 } }
				set_defacto_liege = ROOT
				set_character_flag = against_king
				any_vassal = { set_character_flag = against_king }
				set_character_flag = rebel_leader 
				add_trait = rebel_leader
				add_character_modifier = {
					name = "new_rebel_leader"
					duration = 20
				}	
				e_iron_throne = {
					holder_scope = { 
						opinion = {
							modifier = opinion_traitor
							who = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
